## 请求核对孕期处方 · p12-a-01

主题：健康与照护；情景：孕期处方疑问转交专业人员（pregnancy-prescription-review-request）。

沟通目标：说明孕期用药疑问并约定把当前处方交给医生核对

背景：虚构的诊所沟通练习；Nina 向接待员 Owen 询问如何转交问题，未提供用药建议。

人物：Nina、Owen。

起草：author-phase12-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-a-01-s01 · Nina: I'm calling because I have a question about my prescription during pregnancy.
- p12-a-01-s02 · Owen: Would you like me to send a message to the doctor who issued it?
- p12-a-01-s03 · Nina: Yes. Will the doctor prescribe something different, or do we need to discuss the current medicine first?
- p12-a-01-s04 · Owen: I can't answer that, but I can pass on the question and a copy of the prescription.
- p12-a-01-s05 · Nina: I'll send the copy through the clinic portal. Please ask the doctor to review it.
- p12-a-01-s06 · Owen: I will. This is a request for a review, not a change to your medicine.

目标用法：

- pregnancy · n. · 怀孕；孕期；义项 sense-staged-ox-555d419ed0540d87-p12-a-01；来源 ox-555d419ed0540d87；证据 p12-a-01-s01；词形 pregnancy。
- prescribe · v. · 开具药物处方；义项 sense-staged-ox-c81204218d58f009-p12-a-01；来源 ox-c81204218d58f009；证据 p12-a-01-s03；词形 prescribe。
- prescription · n. · 医生开具的处方；义项 sense-staged-ox-c55fa527774bab8d-p12-a-01；来源 ox-c55fa527774bab8d；证据 p12-a-01-s01；词形 prescription。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The fictional reception call ends with agreement to send the prescription and forward a review request, not with a medication change or a completed clinical review. Title, goal and all evidence statuses preserve that boundary; speakers and clinic roles are consistent.

绑定文件 SHA-256：0fa6cf323a243818bbeafc02ca60ca6ba3ac957ec60a001630aa2a187b46ad45

- ox-c81204218d58f009：accepted；s03 will the doctor prescribe something different uses the base verb after will for issuing medication, posed as a question rather than advice.（p12-a-01-s03）。
- ox-c55fa527774bab8d：accepted；s01 my prescription names the existing medical prescription; the later copy/request keeps the same referent.（p12-a-01-s01）。

## 先了解精神科首次会谈的范围 · p12-a-02

主题：健康与照护；情景：澄清首次精神科接待不要求立即讲述全部经历（psychiatric-intake-first-visit-boundary）。

沟通目标：说明首次会谈的顾虑并决定先阅读本诊所的接待说明

背景：虚构诊所信息咨询练习。Eli 向接待员 Farah 询问首次精神科会谈的安排，不涉及诊断或治疗建议。

人物：Eli、Farah。

起草：author-phase12-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-a-02-s01 · Eli: I'm reluctant to book a psychiatric appointment because I don't want to explain my whole life at once.
- p12-a-02-s02 · Farah: Let me reassure you about our first meeting: it can start with what you want help with now.
- p12-a-02-s03 · Eli: Could I read something about the process before deciding?
- p12-a-02-s04 · Farah: Certainly. Our introduction describes the first meeting and the questions you can ask.
- p12-a-02-s05 · Eli: Please send that. I'd rather read it before choosing a date.
- p12-a-02-s06 · Farah: I'll email it to you. You don't need to book during this call.

目标用法：

- psychiatric · adj. · 精神科的；义项 sense-staged-ox-fadb2ce760460ec9-p12-a-02；来源 ox-fadb2ce760460ec9；证据 p12-a-02-s01；词形 psychiatric。
- reluctant · adj. · 不情愿的；犹豫的；义项 sense-staged-ox-0244a76d00c649ed-p12-a-02；来源 ox-0244a76d00c649ed；证据 p12-a-02-s01；词形 reluctant。
- reassure · v. · 使放心；消除顾虑；义项 sense-staged-ox-12c6a56cee465c93-p12-a-02；来源 ox-12c6a56cee465c93；证据 p12-a-02-s02；词形 reassure。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The hesitant caller asks to read the clinic introduction before selecting an appointment. The receptionist promises an email and expressly does not require booking now. The title and evidence accurately discuss first-visit scope without claiming diagnosis, delivery or a chosen date.

- ox-fadb2ce760460ec9：accepted；s01 psychiatric modifies appointment and correctly identifies the specialty.（p12-a-02-s01）。
- ox-0244a76d00c649ed：accepted；s01 reluctant to book expresses hesitation with a natural adjective-plus-infinitive construction.（p12-a-02-s01）。
- ox-12c6a56cee465c93：accepted；s02 reassure you about our first meeting uses a transitive verb for reducing the caller’s stated concern.（p12-a-02-s02）。

## 限定初步问卷报告的结论 · p12-a-03

主题：研究与论证；情景：限定初步健康问卷的样本与预防结论（preliminary-survey-prevention-claim-scope）。

沟通目标：核对问卷样本范围并约定删除未测量的预防效果结论

背景：两名学生讨论虚构健康调查练习的数据；不是现实医学研究或健康建议。

人物：Amir、Tess。

起草：author-phase12-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-a-03-s01 · Amir: These preliminary results show that most respondents reported poor sleep.
- p12-a-03-s02 · Tess: But the respondents were predominantly staff on the night shift. We can't describe everyone at the company.
- p12-a-03-s03 · Amir: Then I'll report the prevalence of poor sleep in this sample, not in the whole workforce.
- p12-a-03-s04 · Tess: What about your sentence saying the new timetable helps with prevention?
- p12-a-03-s05 · Amir: We didn't measure that. I'll remove it and describe who answered the survey.
- p12-a-03-s06 · Tess: Good. Let's keep the sample description beside the results.

目标用法：

- preliminary · adj. · 初步的；义项 sense-staged-ox-c316c19ee50d01df-p12-a-03；来源 ox-c316c19ee50d01df；证据 p12-a-03-s01；词形 preliminary。
- prevalence · n. · 某情况在群体中的普遍程度；义项 sense-staged-ox-fb9ebf905f390a91-p12-a-03；来源 ox-fb9ebf905f390a91；证据 p12-a-03-s03；词形 prevalence。
- prevention · n. · 预防；义项 sense-staged-ox-2f85558af63e1b87-p12-a-03；来源 ox-2f85558af63e1b87；证据 p12-a-03-s04；词形 prevention。
- predominantly · adv. · 主要地；占大多数地；义项 sense-staged-ox-9d9f2b9720cda0a4-p12-a-03；来源 ox-9d9f2b9720cda0a4；证据 p12-a-03-s02；词形 predominantly。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The students identify a night-shift-heavy fictional sample, restrict the prevalence claim and agree to remove an unmeasured prevention claim. Sample versus workforce and promised editing versus completed editing remain distinct throughout the metadata and evidence.

- ox-fb9ebf905f390a91：accepted；s03 prevalence of poor sleep in this sample uses the noun for frequency within a bounded group.（p12-a-03-s03）。
- ox-2f85558af63e1b87：accepted；s04 helps with prevention uses the noun for prevention of the sleep problem discussed; it is an unsupported draft claim explicitly questioned and removed, not health advice.（p12-a-03-s04）。

## 为虚构省长采访拟定预算问题 · p12-a-04

主题：社会与公共事务；情景：为虚构省政府采访拟定具体预算问题（fictional-provincial-interview-budget-question）。

沟通目标：区分省级预算承诺与结果证据并拟定具体采访问题

背景：新闻写作课堂中的虚构省政府材料。Mara 与 Joel 准备模拟采访，所述人物、地区与政策均非当前新闻。

人物：Mara、Joel。

起草：author-phase12-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-a-04-s01 · Mara: The premier promises greater prosperity in this speech. Is that enough for our opening question?
- p12-a-04-s02 · Joel: It's too broad. What does the provincial budget actually fund?
- p12-a-04-s03 · Mara: The exercise says the province will pay for three bus routes, but it gives no results yet.
- p12-a-04-s04 · Joel: Then ask which communities those routes serve and how success will be measured.
- p12-a-04-s05 · Mara: So: 'Which communities will get the routes, and what would count as success?'
- p12-a-04-s06 · Joel: Yes. That tests the promise without treating it as an achieved result.

目标用法：

- premier · n. · 省级政府首脑；省长；义项 sense-staged-ox-9c4800577fd3e3bc-p12-a-04；来源 ox-9c4800577fd3e3bc；证据 p12-a-04-s01；词形 premier。
- province · n. · 省；义项 sense-staged-ox-56b4d49ac8b29884-p12-a-04；来源 ox-56b4d49ac8b29884；证据 p12-a-04-s03；词形 province。
- provincial · adj. · 省级的；义项 sense-staged-ox-5079a631dd95fa79-p12-a-04；来源 ox-5079a631dd95fa79；证据 p12-a-04-s02；词形 provincial。
- prosperity · n. · 繁荣；义项 sense-staged-ox-a0ef1c380bff6107-p12-a-04；来源 ox-a0ef1c380bff6107；证据 p12-a-04-s01；词形 prosperity。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The fictional provincial context establishes premier as the province’s government head. The speakers distinguish a funding promise from absent results and formulate an actual interview question in s05, endorsed in s06. No real news or achieved policy outcome is asserted.

## 确认主席空缺期间的会议主持人 · p12-a-05

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：确认社团主席辞职后的临时会议主持人（club-presidency-vacancy-meeting-chair）。

沟通目标：确认社团主席职位空缺并约定由副主席主持临时会议

背景：业余摄影社团秘书 Lena 与副主席 Victor 核对已收到的辞职信及临时会议安排。

人物：Lena、Victor。

起草：author-phase12-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-a-05-s01 · Lena: We've received Ada's resignation, so the club presidency is vacant.
- p12-a-05-s02 · Victor: Does our handbook say who should preside at the meeting tonight?
- p12-a-05-s03 · Lena: Yes. The vice-chair takes that role while the members arrange an election.
- p12-a-05-s04 · Victor: That's me, then. I'll chair tonight's discussion, but I'm not taking over as permanent president.
- p12-a-05-s05 · Lena: Exactly. I'll put the election arrangements on the agenda.
- p12-a-05-s06 · Victor: Please do. I'll open the meeting by explaining the temporary arrangement.

目标用法：

- presidency · n. · 主席职位；义项 sense-staged-ox-5296aeb898bdb84f-p12-a-05；来源 ox-5296aeb898bdb84f；证据 p12-a-05-s01；词形 presidency。
- preside · v. · 主持会议；义项 sense-staged-ox-aa561fbaf0333096-p12-a-05；来源 ox-aa561fbaf0333096；证据 p12-a-05-s02；词形 preside。
- resignation · n. · 辞职；辞职信；义项 sense-staged-ox-c4100102f3bf6a1e-p12-a-05；来源 ox-c4100102f3bf6a1e；证据 p12-a-05-s01；词形 resignation。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The received resignation creates a stated vacancy; the handbook assigns the vice-chair temporary meeting leadership and Victor explicitly accepts. The context establishes secretary and vice-chair roles, and neither title nor evidence promotes him to permanent president.

## 区分虚构海报的宣传与事实 · p12-a-06

主题：学习与技能；情景：分析虚构历史海报的宣传修辞与证据边界（fictional-poster-rhetoric-evidence-label）。

沟通目标：指出海报的宣传承诺并约定在说明牌上保留归属而不把承诺写成事实

背景：历史课堂使用自编的虚构政治海报。Imani 与 Hugo 编写材料说明，不评价现实政治人物。

人物：Imani、Hugo。

起草：author-phase12-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-a-06-s01 · Imani: This poster seems to proclaim the start of a perfect society.
- p12-a-06-s02 · Hugo: That's revolutionary rhetoric, not a description of what actually happened.
- p12-a-06-s03 · Imani: Should our label call it propaganda?
- p12-a-06-s04 · Hugo: Yes, if we explain its purpose: it tries to win support for the fictional movement.
- p12-a-06-s05 · Imani: I'll write that the poster promises a perfect society, rather than saying the movement created one.
- p12-a-06-s06 · Hugo: That keeps the claim attached to the poster. Use that version.

目标用法：

- propaganda · n. · 旨在影响公众立场的宣传材料；义项 sense-staged-ox-a88eccac29444a85-p12-a-06；来源 ox-a88eccac29444a85；证据 p12-a-06-s03；词形 propaganda。
- proclaim · v. · 公开宣告；义项 sense-staged-ox-46c81a1abc6de5cf-p12-a-06；来源 ox-46c81a1abc6de5cf；证据 p12-a-06-s01；词形 proclaim。
- rhetoric · n. · 具有鼓动性或修饰性的言辞；义项 sense-staged-ox-0cd575ad32a612b2-p12-a-06；来源 ox-0cd575ad32a612b2；证据 p12-a-06-s02；词形 rhetoric。
- revolutionary · adj. · 革命的；义项 sense-staged-ox-b390b9d435c7d5c2-p12-a-06；来源 ox-b390b9d435c7d5c2；证据 p12-a-06-s02；词形 revolutionary。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The classroom poster’s promise is identified as advocacy, not a historical achievement. The speakers agree on a label that attributes the promise to the poster, and all outcome claims correctly leave the label revision pending.

## 核对虚构供水公投的资料边界 · p12-a-07

主题：社会与公共事务；情景：核对虚构供水公投资料与监管问询渠道（fictional-water-ballot-information-scope）。

沟通目标：澄清投票问题未提供的细节并约定向资料列出的监管机构提出问题

背景：公民教育课堂的虚构供水方案。Noor 与 Ben 只核对练习资料，不劝说现实选民或提供法律意见。

人物：Noor、Ben。

- p12-a-07-s03 · Noor: No. The regulatory arrangements are in a separate document that we haven't been given.
- p12-a-07-s04 · Ben: Then we shouldn't invent an answer about prices.
- p12-a-07-s05 · Noor: I'll draft a question for the regulator listed on the sheet: where can we read the proposed rules on charges?
- p12-a-07-s06 · Ben: Agreed. Let's request the missing document before comparing the options.

- p12-a-09-s04 · Bea: Then keep preservation of the fragments separate from the illustration. We aren't proposing physical restoration of the bowl.

- p12-a-09-s06 · Bea: Yes, and leave the actual fragments in their current display.

## 核对虚构庭审笔记的说话人 · p12-a-16

背景：法律英语课堂中，学生 Mina 与 Oscar 整理自编的虚构庭审记录，不涉及现实案件或法律建议。

- p12-a-16-s04 · Oscar: Right. It's an argument from the prosecution at this point, not the court's conclusion.
- p12-a-16-s05 · Mina: I'll identify the speaker beside that line and remove the claim about a court decision.

## 约定专注时段试验的比较方法 · p12-a-20

人物：Arlo、Nia。

起草：author-phase12-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-a-20-s01 · Arlo: I felt more productive when we turned off meeting alerts for an hour.
- p12-a-20-s02 · Nia: That's encouraging, but how will we measure productivity rather than just a feeling?
- p12-a-20-s03 · Arlo: We could count finished tasks.
- p12-a-20-s04 · Nia: Only if the tasks are comparable. A robust comparison should also track work that needs correcting.
- p12-a-20-s05 · Arlo: Let's use the same type of task and record completed items and rework during the trial.
- p12-a-20-s06 · Nia: Agreed. We'll review both measures before deciding whether to keep the quiet hour.

- productive · adj. · 富有成效的；义项 sense-staged-ox-bbdcef362f23d4c7-p12-a-20；来源 ox-bbdcef362f23d4c7；证据 p12-a-20-s01；词形 productive。

The coworkers separate a subjective feeling of productivity from a trial measure. Comparable task types and both completions and rework are explicitly agreed, with a later review before retaining the quiet hour. The scene does not equate raw quantity with proven quality-adjusted improvement or claim results already exist.

- p12-a-21-s04 · Reza: Interest isn't the same as a purchase, and twelve conversations don't establish forty orders a day.
- p12-a-21-s05 · Dana: Then the sales assumption needs to be much more visible.

No certain profit or investment advice is given, and revision remains promised.

主题：文娱与运动；情景：虚构人物介绍的因果边界（fictional-character-separation-portrayal）。

沟通目标：核对分居情节并约定删去缺乏依据的因果暗示

背景：编辑 Nia 与作者 Hugo 讨论一部虚构小说中成年双性恋人物的介绍文字。

人物：Nia、Hugo。

起草：author-phase12-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-b-12-s01 · Nia: This sentence makes her sexuality sound like the cause of the separation.
- p12-b-12-s02 · Hugo: That wasn’t my intention. Her spouse takes a job overseas, and they agree to live apart for a year.
- p12-b-12-s03 · Nia: Then say that. Linking the separation to her being bisexual repeats a stereotype the story itself doesn’t support.
- p12-b-12-s04 · Hugo: I can keep the identity detail elsewhere, without making it an explanation for their living arrangements.
- p12-b-12-s05 · Nia: Exactly. The introduction should follow the events in the chapter.
- p12-b-12-s06 · Hugo: I’ll rewrite these two sentences with the overseas job as the stated reason.

目标用法：

- sexuality · n. · 性取向；义项 sense-staged-ox-d8c2d5cd2da7ffab-p12-b-12；来源 ox-d8c2d5cd2da7ffab；证据 p12-b-12-s01；词形 sexuality。
- separation · n. · 伴侣分居；义项 sense-staged-ox-fdbd660e07b11409-p12-b-12；来源 ox-fdbd660e07b11409；证据 p12-b-12-s01；词形 separation。
- spouse · n. · 配偶；义项 sense-staged-ox-2f16e8e0f9b19e90-p12-b-12；来源 ox-2f16e8e0f9b19e90；证据 p12-b-12-s02；词形 spouse。
- stereotype · n. · 刻板印象；义项 sense-staged-ox-aa4004e131db8544-p12-b-12；来源 ox-aa4004e131db8544；证据 p12-b-12-s03；词形 stereotype。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The fictional adult-character discussion separates stated overseas employment from an unsupported causal stereotype about bisexuality. Both agree to relocate identity information and revise the causal explanation; actual revision remains promised.

- p12-b-13-s04 · Lila: The movement is quiet. Could we try one spotlight and let the audience notice the hands?
- p12-b-13-s05 · Rowan: Yes. I’ll turn off the moving beams for the next run.
- p12-b-13-s06 · Lila: Thanks. Let’s try that version before adding anything else.

## 核对双屏客服培训演示 · p12-b-14

主题：学习与技能；情景：双屏模拟演示的设备与分工（training-demo-two-screen-simulation）。

- p12-b-14-s03 · Ben: Can we simulate a delayed reply on the left while the right conversation continues?
- p12-b-14-s04 · Zoe: We can. I’ll advance the left sequence more slowly during the simulation.
- p12-b-14-s05 · Ben: Do we need any specialized hardware?
- p12-b-14-s06 · Zoe: No. The equipment specification asks for two ordinary screens and a computer, all of which are here.
- p12-b-14-s07 · Ben: Then let’s test both sequences with the fictional messages before the session.
- p12-b-14-s08 · Zoe: Agreed. I’ll take the left sequence, and you can watch the right.

主题：学习与技能；情景：虚构股东案例的证据摘要（fictional-shareholder-case-summary）。

人物：Iris、Adam。

起草：author-phase12-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-b-15-s01 · Iris: Our case says Mara is a shareholder with a forty percent stake in the company.
- p12-b-15-s02 · Adam: That’s substantial. Should we describe her as controlling the company?
- p12-b-15-s03 · Iris: Not from that figure alone. The case doesn’t give us the voting rules.
- p12-b-15-s04 · Adam: It does say the purchase was a strategic decision to gain access to a design partnership.
- p12-b-15-s05 · Iris: Yes, that purpose is stated. We can include it without guessing who controls every vote.
- p12-b-15-s06 · Adam: I’ll separate the ownership figure, the stated purpose, and the missing voting information.

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The fictional business case gives forty percent ownership and a design-partnership purpose but no voting rules. The speakers maintain that information boundary and agree to separate facts from missing control information; no investment recommendation is made.

- p12-b-17-s03 · Bea: The test note says to soak the sample, then let it dry before measuring again.
- p12-b-17-s04 · Lin: There’s a loose strand along this edge already. Let’s record that too, so we don’t blame the test for it.
- p12-b-17-s05 · Bea: Good idea. I’ll write down the size and mark the loose thread in the photo.
- p12-b-17-s06 · Lin: Then we’ll follow the note on just this sample, not the whole piece.

## 认真回应室友对摔门声的不满 · p12-b-18

- p12-b-18-s03 · Tess: Please don’t just shrug. I’m trying to explain why I was upset.

- p12-b-18-s05 · Tess: I don’t need a long explanation. Could you just close it gently when you come in late?
- p12-b-18-s06 · Owen: Yes. I’ll keep a hand on it next time, even if I have to put a bag down.

## 把舞台打斗效果与真实接触分开 · p12-b-19

主题：文娱与运动；情景：戏剧分镜的非接触打斗设计（stage-fight-effects-not-contact）。

沟通目标：核对剧本中的打斗动作并约定交由动作指导设计安全替代表现

背景：成年戏剧学生 Jules 与 Ren 讨论虚构故事分镜，只商量由专业动作指导安排无真实接触的表演，不提供实施伤害的技巧。

人物：Jules、Ren。

起草：author-phase12-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-b-19-s01 · Jules: The script has the guard slap the prisoner, and then the villain tries to stab the guard.
- p12-b-19-s02 · Ren: We’re not making real contact for either action. The fight director needs to design how those moments are shown.
- p12-b-19-s03 · Jules: There’s also a line saying the guard should smash a window while escaping.

- p12-c-23-s02 · Mei: No. This is still a theoretical model; we haven't tested it.
- p12-c-23-s03 · Hugo: And temperature is a variable we haven't included at all.
- p12-c-23-s04 · Mei: Then we should describe the result as a prediction to check, not a confirmed finding.
- p12-c-23-s05 · Hugo: I'll rewrite that sentence and list the missing variable in the limitations.
- p12-c-23-s06 · Mei: Yes. The proposed test can stay in the next-steps section.

- theoretical · adj. · 理论上的；义项 sense-staged-ox-6dd857a2f20a3e7c-p12-c-23；来源 ox-6dd857a2f20a3e7c；证据 p12-c-23-s02；词形 theoretical。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The classroom model is explicitly theoretical, untested and missing temperature; the students agree to change the claimed validity into a testable prediction with limitations. No experimental validation or real engineering advice is claimed.

## 约定系统整理访谈差异再写综合 · p12-c-24

主题：研究与论证；情景：约定系统整理访谈差异再写综合（interview-synthesis-coding-plan）。

沟通目标：承认访谈回答的差异不能被抹平并约定统一编码后再写有范围的综合

背景：两名学生整理已经匿名化的虚构访谈材料，所有回答为课程练习编写。

人物：Noor、Eli。

起草：author-phase12-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-c-24-s01 · Noor: The responses are so varied that my summary sounds like three different projects.
- p12-c-24-s02 · Eli: That's useful information. A synthesis doesn't have to pretend everyone agrees.
- p12-c-24-s03 · Noor: I was about to keep only the most common answer.
- p12-c-24-s04 · Eli: Let's take a systematic approach first: use the same topic labels for each interview.
- p12-c-24-s05 · Noor: And keep a place for answers that don't fit the main pattern?
- p12-c-24-s06 · Eli: Yes. We'll keep those responses in their own column, thereby showing where the interviews differ.
- p12-c-24-s07 · Noor: All right. We'll finish that coding table before writing the synthesis.

- systematic · adj. · 系统的；义项 sense-staged-ox-6edf552af3a49c94-p12-c-24；来源 ox-6edf552af3a49c94；证据 p12-c-24-s04；词形 systematic。

- varied · adj. · 多种多样的；义项 sense-staged-ox-5bda1f27b869f071-p12-c-24；来源 ox-5bda1f27b869f071；证据 p12-c-24-s01；词形 varied。
- thereby · adv. · 由此、从而；义项 sense-staged-ox-df4ce5442f29e250-p12-c-24；来源 ox-df4ce5442f29e250；证据 p12-c-24-s06；词形 thereby。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The students acknowledge different responses, reject reducing them all to the common answer and agree to consistent topic coding plus a column for exceptions before writing the synthesis. The evidence correctly treats the coding/write-up as agreed future work.

## 约定先核对品牌标记许可再决定使用 · p12-c-25

主题：工作与协作；情景：核对品牌标记许可后再决定设计统一方式（brand-trademark-draft-permission）。

沟通目标：区分视觉草稿与标记使用许可并约定先询问许可再决定是否加入标记

背景：虚构设计委托沟通。设计师讨论客户发送的品牌草图，不作商标法律判断。

起草：author-phase12-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-c-25-s01 · Asha: This blue emblem would help unify the pages. Is it the client's trademark?
- p12-c-25-s02 · Tom: That's what the file name says, but the email doesn't explain whether we're allowed to use it in this project.
- p12-c-25-s03 · Asha: Then the visual transformation can wait. I'd rather check than copy it onto every page.
- p12-c-25-s04 · Tom: Agreed. The draft layout can stay as it is without the emblem.
- p12-c-25-s05 · Asha: I'll ask the client about permission first, then we can make the pages consistent if they confirm it.
- p12-c-25-s06 · Tom: Good. I'll keep that version separate from the working draft.

The design team distinguishes a file name describing a trademark from actual permission. They agree to ask the client first and leave the working layout without the emblem; adding it and unifying pages is expressly conditional on confirmation. No legal clearance or completed redesign is asserted.

- p12-c-27-s03 · Gabe: It was surprisingly thought-provoking for such a short piece.
- p12-c-27-s04 · Sana: The other essay is good too, but its opening is much more serious.
- p12-c-27-s05 · Gabe: Let's begin with the funny one. People can start with a favourite line and move into the questions it raises.

## 为神学参访确认礼拜观察边界 · p12-c-32

主题：学习与技能；情景：为神学参访确认礼拜观察边界（theology-visit-worship-boundary）。

沟通目标：区分课程参访与参加礼拜并约定按接待人员提示安静观察

背景：虚构神学课程学生联系一处愿意接待参访的宗教场所，只讨论参访礼仪与自主选择，不评价信仰优劣。

人物：Ivo、Ms Hart。

起草：author-phase12-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-c-32-s01 · Ivo: Our theology class is visiting on Friday. Are we expected to join the worship?
- p12-c-32-s02 · Ms Hart: No. You're welcome to observe from the rear seats, but participation is entirely your choice.
- p12-c-32-s03 · Ivo: Thank you. We want to learn without getting in anyone's way.
- p12-c-32-s04 · Ms Hart: That's welcome. We value tolerance here, so visitors don't have to share our beliefs. Please save questions for the conversation afterwards.
- p12-c-32-s05 · Ivo: Of course. We'll sit where you direct us and keep our questions until the end.
- p12-c-32-s06 · Ms Hart: I'll meet your group at the entrance and show you the seats.

- theology · n. · 神学；义项 sense-staged-ox-4757fc5d45ec9e20-p12-c-32；来源 ox-4757fc5d45ec9e20；证据 p12-c-32-s01；词形 theology。

- tolerance · n. · 包容、尊重差异；义项 sense-staged-ox-d6a1a8ee7b85c0e8-p12-c-32；来源 ox-d6a1a8ee7b85c0e8；证据 p12-c-32-s04；词形 tolerance。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The host explicitly permits observation without religious participation. The student accepts directed seating and deferred questions, and the host offers to meet the group. These are specific visit arrangements rather than claims about all religious communities or beliefs.

## 为争执中的侮辱道歉并商定暂停信号 · p12-c-33

主题：家庭与人际；情景：为争执中的侮辱道歉并商定暂停信号（shared-flat-argument-apology）。

沟通目标：承认争吵中的言语伤害并道歉并约定使用暂停信号而不是继续喊叫

背景：两位成年室友在一次口角后谈论彼此的沟通方式；场景为虚构，无针对真实人物的指控。

人物：Finn、Lara。

起草：author-phase12-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-c-33-s01 · Finn: I'm sorry about the vicious things I said last night. Being annoyed doesn't excuse them.
- p12-c-33-s02 · Lara: Thank you for saying that. I can discuss a disagreement, but I won't tolerate being insulted.
- p12-c-33-s03 · Finn: I understand. I was wrong to make it personal.
- p12-c-33-s04 · Lara: If either of us starts to yell, can we say 'pause' and stop the conversation for a while?
- p12-c-33-s05 · Finn: Yes. Either person can call it, and we can come back when we're ready to speak calmly.
- p12-c-33-s06 · Lara: That's a better plan than trying to win the argument.

目标用法：

- tolerate · v. · 容忍；义项 sense-staged-ox-74ca95245a2d7192-p12-c-33；来源 ox-74ca95245a2d7192；证据 p12-c-33-s02；词形 tolerate。
- yell · v. · 大喊；义项 sense-staged-ox-8bd03a6542eaa13e-p12-c-33；来源 ox-8bd03a6542eaa13e；证据 p12-c-33-s04；词形 yell。
- vicious · adj. · 恶毒的；义项 sense-staged-ox-2949e2b88de4b65b-p12-c-33；来源 ox-2949e2b88de4b65b；证据 p12-c-33-s01；词形 vicious。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Finn directly apologises and takes responsibility; Lara accepts the acknowledgement while maintaining a boundary against insults. Their pause signal receives explicit reciprocal agreement. The evidence correctly separates the completed apology from an agreed future communication plan.

背景：写作课编辑虚构村民家史。人物、家庭经历和作物均为练习设定，不涉及真实个人资料或真实战事。

- p12-c-34-s03 · Alma: So 'she returned from the war' is simply the wrong subject.
- p12-c-34-s04 · Chris: Yes. They later grew tobacco together, but that farming experience doesn't change who served.
- p12-c-34-s05 · Alma: I'll name her husband in the military-history sentence, then use 'they' for the farm.
- p12-c-34-s06 · Chris: Good. Keep her own memories of working there rather than turning the whole piece into his biography.
- p12-c-34-s07 · Alma: I will. The corrected paragraph should make both roles clear.

- p12-c-35-s05 · Hana: Would you read it once I've written those points down?

- p12-c-36-s04 · Leah: We have the gold scene, but the loyalty is only mentioned in the final paragraph.

## 按证据限制削弱报告建议的力度 · p12-c-38

主题：研究与论证；情景：按证据限制削弱报告建议的力度（report-recommendation-strength）。

沟通目标：区分局部证据缺陷与全盘否定并约定调低建议强度并说明适用范围

背景：两位研究课学生讨论虚构活动评估报告，样本只有六次访谈；不作现实政策或专业决策。

人物：Tobin、Yara。

起草：author-phase12-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-c-38-s01 · Tobin: Does the small sample undermine the whole report?
- p12-c-38-s02 · Yara: Not wholly. The six interviews still contain useful observations; they just don't represent everyone.
- p12-c-38-s03 · Tobin: Then the problem is our recommendation to change every branch at once.
- p12-c-38-s04 · Yara: Yes. We should weaken that recommendation and suggest a small pilot instead.
- p12-c-38-s05 · Tobin: I'll make that change and state exactly who we interviewed.
- p12-c-38-s06 · Yara: That keeps the useful evidence without making it carry more weight than it can.

- undermine · v. · 削弱可信度；义项 sense-staged-ox-df20565b7f20811e-p12-c-38；来源 ox-df20565b7f20811e；证据 p12-c-38-s01；词形 undermine。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The students preserve useful six-interview observations while rejecting universal rollout based on them. Both agree to a smaller pilot recommendation and explicit sample scope, accurately distinguishing limited evidence from wholesale invalidity.

- p12-c-41-s01 · Marta: We have ninety registrations. That's unprecedented for this series; we've never had more than sixty.
- p12-c-41-s02 · Ian: The internal threshold for a venue review is eighty, isn't it?
- p12-c-41-s03 · Marta: Yes. Do these figures warrant moving the talk to a larger hall?
- p12-c-41-s04 · Ian: They warrant asking, not assuming. First, let's check for duplicate registrations.
- p12-c-41-s05 · Marta: Then I'll send the verified count to the venue and request a capacity review.
- p12-c-41-s06 · Ian: Good. We won't announce a new room until they've replied.

目标用法：

- threshold · n. · 阈值；义项 sense-staged-ox-309515ada051034d-p12-c-41；来源 ox-309515ada051034d；证据 p12-c-41-s02；词形 threshold。
- warrant · v. · 有理由采取、使有必要；义项 sense-staged-ox-8081edfd848fa89f-p12-c-41；来源 ox-8081edfd848fa89f；证据 p12-c-41-s03；词形 warrant。
- unprecedented · adj. · 前所未有的；义项 sense-staged-ox-1b2d62addfc6be00-p12-c-41；来源 ox-1b2d62addfc6be00；证据 p12-c-41-s01；词形 unprecedented。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The raw ninety registrations exceed the stated internal review threshold of eighty, but the speakers distinguish that from a verified person-count or permission to move rooms. They agree to deduplicate first, request venue review and await a reply before announcing a new hall.

## 暂缓开展手工项目直到了解需求 · p12-c-42

主题：工作与协作；情景：暂缓开展手工项目直到了解需求（craft-venture-demand-check）。

沟通目标：区分项目吸引力与可行性证据并约定先访谈潜在顾客再决定是否开展

背景：虚构手工项目规划沟通，不涉及真实投资、付款或个人财务建议。

人物：Jade、Morgan。

起草：author-phase12-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-c-42-s01 · Jade: The low stall fee might tempt us to rush into this little craft venture.
- p12-c-42-s02 · Morgan: I like the idea, but we don't yet know whether it's viable.
- p12-c-42-s03 · Jade: Exactly. Friends saying the bowls are pretty isn't the same as customers wanting to buy them.
- p12-c-42-s04 · Morgan: Let's talk to a few potential customers about what they actually need before we commit to a stall.
- p12-c-42-s05 · Jade: And treat those conversations as a first check, not a sales forecast.
- p12-c-42-s06 · Morgan: Agreed. We'll hold off on booking until we've learned something useful.

目标用法：

- tempt · v. · 诱使；义项 sense-staged-ox-c2ef5e7b416bb276-p12-c-42；来源 ox-c2ef5e7b416bb276；证据 p12-c-42-s01；词形 tempt。
- venture · n. · 新项目、创业项目；义项 sense-staged-ox-ea1ef9651313b287-p12-c-42；来源 ox-ea1ef9651313b287；证据 p12-c-42-s01；词形 venture。
- viable · adj. · 可行的；义项 sense-staged-ox-7c6137adaa71521a-p12-c-42；来源 ox-7c6137adaa71521a；证据 p12-c-42-s02；词形 viable。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The partners distinguish attractive low fees and compliments from evidence of purchasing demand. They jointly defer the commercial stall commitment until preliminary customer conversations, and explicitly avoid presenting those conversations as a sales forecast.

## 约定申请单次舞蹈健身试办 · p12-c-43

主题：文娱与运动；情景：约定申请单次舞蹈健身试办（dance-workout-room-pilot）。

沟通目标：讨论可供试办使用的房间并约定先申请单次试办而非固定课程

背景：虚构休闲活动策划。组织者只讨论房间和活动试办，不提供具体运动或医疗指导。

人物：Zoe、Patrick。

起草：author-phase12-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-c-43-s01 · Zoe: That dance workout last month had such a vibrant atmosphere. Could we try a small session here?
- p12-c-43-s02 · Patrick: We might utilize the side room on a quiet afternoon, but we'd need the manager's approval.
- p12-c-43-s03 · Zoe: I wouldn't want to promise a weekly class before we've tried it.
- p12-c-43-s04 · Patrick: Then let's ask for one short pilot session and invite feedback afterwards.
- p12-c-43-s05 · Zoe: Yes. One session in the request, with no standing booking.
- p12-c-43-s06 · Patrick: I'll draft it that way and send it to you to check before it goes to the manager.

目标用法：

- workout · n. · 锻炼活动；义项 sense-staged-ox-ef99f388d5d7d5c5-p12-c-43；来源 ox-ef99f388d5d7d5c5；证据 p12-c-43-s01；词形 workout。
- utilize · v. · 利用；义项 sense-staged-ox-f6a3974bce13491e-p12-c-43；来源 ox-f6a3974bce13491e；证据 p12-c-43-s02；词形 utilize。
- vibrant · adj. · 充满活力的；义项 sense-staged-ox-886f17230720f9b7-p12-c-43；来源 ox-886f17230720f9b7；证据 p12-c-43-s01；词形 vibrant。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The organisers consider using a side room only with manager approval. They explicitly agree to request a single short dance-workout pilot, not a weekly booking, and arrange draft review before submission. Prior enjoyment is not treated as approval or proof the new session will happen.

## 用交接日志替代含糊的口头安排 · p12-c-44

主题：工作与协作；情景：用交接日志替代含糊的口头安排（shift-log-written-handover）。

沟通目标：指出口头交接的含糊之处并约定先试用可核对的书面交接流程

背景：两名工作同事改进班次交接，讨论一个尚未实施的日志试用办法。

人物：Freya、Will。

起草：author-phase12-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p12-c-44-s01 · Freya: Our verbal handover is too vague. Yesterday, I couldn't tell which deliveries were still waiting.
- p12-c-44-s02 · Will: I said the paperwork was ready, but I didn't say that the boxes hadn't arrived.
- p12-c-44-s03 · Freya: We need a system whereby each outgoing shift lists what's finished and what's still open.
- p12-c-44-s04 · Will: I could update the log whilst the incoming person checks the outstanding items.
- p12-c-44-s05 · Freya: Then both people can ask about anything that doesn't match.
- p12-c-44-s06 · Will: Shall we try that for a week rather than make it a permanent rule straight away?
- p12-c-44-s07 · Freya: Yes. We'll review the log together next Friday and see what needs changing.

目标用法：

- vague · adj. · 含糊的；义项 sense-staged-ox-28224f6cd3d09726-p12-c-44；来源 ox-28224f6cd3d09726；证据 p12-c-44-s01；词形 vague。
- verbal · adj. · 口头的；义项 sense-staged-ox-f2d13e062cb489e3-p12-c-44；来源 ox-f2d13e062cb489e3；证据 p12-c-44-s01；词形 verbal。
- whereby · adv. · 借以、通过这种方式；义项 sense-staged-ox-f09cdde472b63995-p12-c-44；来源 ox-f09cdde472b63995；证据 p12-c-44-s03；词形 whereby。
- whilst · conj. · 在……的同时；义项 sense-staged-ox-d1bc2da63fecefce-p12-c-44；来源 ox-d1bc2da63fecefce；证据 p12-c-44-s04；词形 whilst。

审阅者：reviewer-phase12-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The coworkers identify a specific ambiguity between ready paperwork and undelivered boxes, then agree to a written outgoing/incoming check with questions. They explicitly limit it to a one-week trial and Friday review, matching the future implementation and non-permanent status in the evidence.
